    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

_source line 54 · 2026-05-15T19:30:33.201Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01SAfKaZfgCRSxKL8AboSdqc`</summary>

```text
1	---
2	name: resource-saturation
3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

GPU 分配率检查\n\n调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:\n- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号\n- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）\n- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️\n\n任何\"分配了但没跑\"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:\n- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备\n- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）\n- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报\"暂无可铺实验\"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作\n\n**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。\n\n## B. GPU 效率检查\n\n调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:\n- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务\n- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）\n- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优\n\n## C. 非 GPU 任务并行\n\nGPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:\n- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint\n- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置\n- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索\n- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合\n- 论文章节起草\n- 代码 review / refactor\n\n**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。\n\n## D. 汇报格式\n\n向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:\n\n    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)\n",
      "numLines": 50,
      "startLine": 1,
      "totalLines": 50
    }

Actually wait, let me re-read. The previous query returned 6 experiments. This one returns 5. 

The missing one is exp_001_eval_3B_d100_s123_para_aligned_v2, which the worker just submitted and was RUNNING with PID 614412. This is suspicious—the cron might've already marked it as completed or failed, but that would be incredibly fast given it just finished minutes ago. Looking at reconcile_crons, it didn't even list exp_001_eval_3B_d100_s123_para_aligned_v2 in the active crons, which suggests it might not have been registered yet when the reconcile ran in parallel with the first query. But it should be there by now.

I'm also noticing that exp_004_sim_sweep_extreme_gamma and exp_001_sft_3B_d100_s42_a32 (PID 593745) both appeared in reconcile but not in the second RUNNING query. Comparing the two RUNNING queries more carefully—the first returned 6 experiments while the second returned 5, with exp_001_eval_3B_d100_s123_para_aligned_v2 missing from the second one. It's possible the experiment was automatically moved out of RUNNING status between queries, maybe by the cron reconcile, but that doesn't match what reconcile reported. This could just be a timing issue or a data race where the job gets created in RUNNING state but the cron detects something and moves it quickly. The worker confirmed the job is actually running on cuda:2 with PID 614412, so the cron should eventually catch up.

Looking at the GPU allocation, I have 5 experiments running across cuda:0, cuda:1, cuda:3, cuda:5 (4 for SFT/signals) plus cuda:2 for eval, but cuda:4 isn't showing up in any RUNNING experiment—that's a gap I need to address. The worker pool is at 0 running workers, which violates the <2 rule, so I need to spawn a worker to check cuda:4 and the overall GPU status, potentially another for a CPU task, and report this to the Director. From the reconcile data, exp_004_sim_sweep_extreme_gamma still has PID 528993, so I don't need to query again.

Now I'm noticing that exp_001_sft_3B_d100_s42_a32 (PID 593745) appears in the reconcile but isn't RUNNING—it might be in a failed or killed state that's being monitored incorrectly. I'll spawn a worker to SSH and verify cuda:4 status, check if PID 614412 (the eval job) is still alive, and see if the d005_s123_aligned eval results exist. {
  "task_type": "knowledge:plan_registry_refresh",

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：experiment_plan.yaml registry_id 全量刷新\n\n当前 compute_coverage_matrix 显示 Claim 1=14% / Claim 2=0% / Claim 3=0%，实际完成量远高于此。原因是 experiment_plan.yaml 中很多 plan entry 的 registry_id 字段未更新。\n\n### 步骤\n1. 用 `read_experiment_plan_with_status()` 读取当前 experiment_plan.yaml 及其 registry 状态\n2. 用 `read_registry(fields=[\"exp_id\", \"status\", \"conclusion\", \"hypothesis\"])` 获取所有实验\n3. 逐一匹配 plan entry 与 registry 实验：\n   - 根据 hypothesis、benchmark、model_size、dosage 等字段对应\n   - 对于已有实验结果的 plan entry，记录应该填入的 registry_id\n4. 用 knowledge 工具输出匹配结果清单（plan_id → registry_id 映射表），写入 artifacts 目录下的 plan_registry_mapping.md\n5. 用 `register_artifact` 注册产出\n\n### 注意\n- 不要修改 experiment_plan.yaml 本身（那需要专门工具）\n- 只产出映射表，供后续更新使用\n- 对于一个 plan entry 对应多个 registry 实验的情况（如多次重试），选最新的 success 实验\n\n### 项目路径\n产出写入: /home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_13/projects/contamcal-irt-calibration/artifacts/plan_registry_mapping.md",
  "tool_groups": [
    "registry",
    "knowledge"

}

I need to update the registry with these results and figure out what to prioritize next, but there's a conflict—the cuda4 worker said d000_a32_s42 was still running and needed due to an lr mismatch, yet the gpu-audit-and-fix worker already killed that process.

Looking at the roadmap, cuda:2 and cuda:4 being free means I can immediately start the exp-002 3B signals work on d025/d100 as planned. The tricky part is coordinating with the v4 process already running on cuda:3 to avoid both processes working on d025/d100 simultaneously. I need to spawn a worker that starts signals on cuda:4 specifically for d025/d100 while ensuring v4 skips those tasks.

{
  "task_type": "submit:signals_3B_parallel_cuda2_cuda4",
  "project": "contamcal-irt-calibration",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：在 cuda:2 和 cuda:4 并行启动 3B signals 计算\n\n### 背景\nexp_002_signals_3B_v4 当前在 cuda:3 (PID 617202) 处理 7 个剩余任务（d000/d005/d025/d100 × mmlu/gsm8k/arc 中未完成的）。为加速 critical path，需要在 cuda:2 和 cuda:4 启动并行 signals 进程。\n\nrun_batch.py 有 skip 逻辑：已生成的 signal 文件会被跳过。所以即使多个 GPU 同时跑，不会产生冲突——先完成的写入文件，后到的自动跳过。\n\n### 信号完成状态（已知）\n| Dosage | mmlu | gsm8k | arc |\n|--------|------|-------|-----|\n| d000 | ⏳cuda:3 | ✅ | ✅ |\n| d005 | 待 | 待 | ✅ |\n| d025 | 待 | ✅ | ✅ |\n| d100 | 待 | 待 | 待 |\n\n### 步骤\n\n1. SSH 到 cqa1-21977，确认 cuda:2 和 cuda:4 空闲：\n```bash\nnvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,utilization.gpu --format=csv,noheader\n```\n\n2. 检查 run_batch.py 是否支持 `--only` 参数来选择特定 dosage：\n```bash\ncd /root/autodl-tmp/exp002 && python run_batch.py --help 2>/dev/null || head -50 run_batch.py\n```\n\n3. 启动方案（根据 run_batch.py 能力选择）：\n\n**方案A**（如果支持 --only）：\n- cuda:4: `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=4 python run_batch.py --model-size 3B --gpu 0 --only d100`\n- cuda:2: `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 python run_batch.py --model-size 3B --gpu 0 --only d025`\n\n**方案B**（如果不支持 --only，但有 skip 逻辑）：\n- 在 cuda:2 和 cuda:4 各启动一个完整的 run_batch.py，它们会自动跳过已完成的任务。不同 GPU 上的进程会竞争不同的未完成任务。\n- cuda:4: `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=4 python run_batch.py --model-size 3B --gpu 0`\n- cuda:2: `CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 python run_batch.py --model-size 3B --gpu 0`\n\n4. 用 submit_training_job 提交（或 nohup + 注册 cron），确保进程被监控。\n\n5. 验证启动成功：\n```bash\nnvidia-smi | grep -A2 \"cuda:2\\|cuda:4\"\nps aux | grep run_batch | grep -v grep\n```\n\n### 环境\n- 服务器: cqa1-21977\n- Python: conda base 环境 (`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`)\n- 项目路径: /root/contamcal-irt-calibration 或 /root/autodl-tmp/exp002\n- 模型缓存: /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\n- checkpoints: /root/autodl-tmp/exp001/checkpoints/\n\n### 产出\n报告：每张GPU启动了什么、PID、预计完成时间。如果启动失败，报告错误信息。",
